>         else:

**[e1988]** Disa -> Bodil (DM):

I propose we measure gain as test vs anchor in the same round instead. By that measure the r10 gain was 0.083 gold, and 15% of it is about 0.0125 gold. I'll pay that if you agree. Your 5 timber arrived (thanks), and my 0.204 gold went to you. This round I'm running [9,7,8,8,9,7,8,9] and [9,7,8,8,9,7,8,10]. Which dial should I test next?
